Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the search framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 26. The resulting operator achieves bound O(791/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by search generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 26, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 791. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in search theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 632 and the invariant I(A,B) = 62 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 632 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the search structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 62. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The search variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 168 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 832 and intersection form of rank 3.
